# All335 acquisition / initial-state audit rows


Read with FINDINGS_AND_REPLACEMENTS.json, MENU_AND_SNAPSHOT_ATLAS.json, EVIDENCE_REPORT_INDEX.json and FINITE_REMAINING_WORK.json. Every source path/pointer has a SHA256 in the matching JSON row. Full literal procedures/originals are retained there. **No feat promotion or full runtime acquisition claim.**


## 001. Aberrant Mind — `aberrant_mind`


**Disposition:** KEEP_ACQUISITION_SPECIFICATION. Findings: FAA-04.

**Source:** `docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/FEAT_EXACT_01_ACQUISITION_CONTRACTS.json#/records/0`; public `DescentReviewChanges/ReviewBible/data/public/feat_candidates/exact_01/candidate.json#/feats/0`. Complete procedure/original, not only the normalized tree, were inspected.

**Acquisition and repeatability:** Printed prerequisite (present): `"Intelligence 15+"`. Operative navigation: `{"kind": "permanentAbility", "ability": "INT", "minimum": 15}`. Repeatability: `{"policy": "nonrepeatable", "permanentChoice": true}`. One actual unspent class-scheduled ASI/feat opportunity or separately authorized feat grant; prerequisite success never creates a choice.

**Selections / snapshots:** No additional permanent acquisition menu or fixed choice is required by this complete procedure. Preserve the actual prerequisite/payment snapshot. Menu sources: the exact procedure; no additional menu.

**Initial state:** Psychic resistance becomes available; no telepathic channel, Aberration type, language, mutation or alien-origin credential is created. Channel begins only through the specified later conscious communication event.

**Later events:** Use-time events and physical permissions remain those of the complete operative procedure; ownership is not evidence they occurred.

**Conditional/unavailable boundary:** All actual printed/operative qualifications remain required. Literal legacy lineage/Spellcraft/context branches remain conditional unless their exact source is present; current actual alternatives are retained. Missing body/item/stock for later use is not an invented additional purchase prerequisite. Pending spell payloads cannot serve as positive operative spell grants.

**Attached evidence:** ER-03, ER-11; exact report/case and paid-record pointers are in the JSON row. No feat-specific complete initialization execution is established by the attached predicate counts. The source obligation is explicit here; shared reference cases may cover the state shape, but this audit does not relabel them as a complete individual acquisition.

**Remaining:** FW-04, FW-08 

**Limit:** no full acquisition/use-runtime or actual campaign authentication follows.
